(c) seed luse `DISPLAY=:1` + `XAUTHORITY=/run/user/1000/gdm/Xauthority` are **bare literals** (NOT in the 4-placeholder substitution set) → GDM-less fresh box mismatch (dup 251-02/04) = GAP/RISK. 5 remediation items (1 CRITICAL: pin get.livinity.io mapping; 2 HIGH: Path C no-seed + Path B CHANGEME; 2 MEDIUM: silent no-op visibility + DISPLAY/XAUTHORITY substitution) for the 251-09 backlog. - ✅ **251-06 DONE** (commit 2c31d5bb) -- Systemd & env-delivery portability audit. Verdict: WITHOUT the hand-made redis-env.conf drop-in, a fresh box gives the luse process a Redis URL ONLY via the in-repo /opt/livos/.env file fallback (server.ts:124). liv-assistant.service ships with NO EnvironmentFile and NO REDIS_URL/LUSE_REDIS_URL (only PATH/HOME/MCP_TIMEOUT, systemd/liv-assistant.service:13,17,19) so aioncore->claude->luse children inherit NOTHING -- unlike livos.service + liv-core/worker/memory which all carry EnvironmentFile=/opt/livos/.env (deploy-livinityd.sh:1480,1572). The per-MCP env block that DOES inject LUSE_REDIS_URL (update.sh:731,737) sits inside `if [[ -n "$_ID" ]]` (update.sh:725) -- only patches a PRE-EXISTING AionUi entry; the first-create (Phase 241 seed) POST exists in NO shell script (only the comment at update.sh:718), so the loop is a no-op on a clean box. redis-env.conf and any *.service.d drop-in confirmed absent everywhere (0 grep matches across scripts/ systemd/ update.sh install.sh) -- pure live-only artifact. Fallback works only on Path A (real REDIS_URL in /opt/livos/.env, :988); breaks on Path B (CHANGEME) and any non-/opt/livos root. RECOMMENDATION: add EnvironmentFile=-/opt/livos/.env to the committed liv-assistant.service so children inherit REDIS_URL (resolveLuseRedisUrl step 2) -- one line, keeps secret file-resident/per-install; NEVER productize the literal drop-in. Secondary: encode the AionUi luse MCP first-create POST. 4 GAP/RISK items for the 251-09 backlog (HIGH severity / LOW effort for the unit fix). The daemon's data path IS parameterized (`cli.ts:59` `--data-directory` → `index.ts:400` `path.resolve` → JWT/store/backups all build off `dataDirectory`; installer derives it from env-overridable `_DLD_LIVOS_DATA_DIR` at `deploy-livinityd.sh:1461`), **BUT** (1) the NEW luse Redis fallback `['/opt/livos/.env','/opt/livos/livos/.env']` (`server.ts:124`, commit `b4f2a345`) and the sandbox uploads allowlist `/opt/livos/data/uploads/<userId>/` (`tools.ts:454`) **re-hardcode the root, ignoring `dataDirectory`** → MISMATCH: a moved data dir → luse `redis===null` fail-closed + `computer_read_file` sandbox over-block; AND (2) the installer root `_DLD_LIVOS_DIR="/opt/livos"` (`:61`) is itself a **bare literal with NO `${VAR:-default}`** unlike its neighbours `_DLD_LIVOS_USER` (`:79`) / `_DLD_DESKTOP_USER` (`:85`). Installer evidence: `_dld_create_desktop_user` (`deploy-livinityd.sh:276-335`) hard-creates `bruce`/uid-1000 by default but is parameterizable (`_DLD_DESKTOP_USER` :279) and **uid can auto-assign** if 1000 is taken (:299-302). 4 portable fixes for 251-09 backlog. - ✅ **251-02 DONE** (commit `42db9429`) — Luse display backend audit. **Verdict: `computer_create_display` with DEFAULT args FAILS on a fresh install.** Default mode = `xephyr` (`display-manager.ts:216` + `tools.ts:1028`) but **no installer apt list installs `xserver-xephyr`** (only `xvfb`; `deploy-livinityd.sh:523`, `update.sh:369`). ## Previous Position (v44 — Phase 248 Plan 03 ✅ SHIPPED — display TTL GC: 1h sweep / 4h idle threshold + owner-impersonation lift + boot wiring in mcp/server.ts main() with beforeExit handler; 8/8 vitest GREEN; 23/23 across displays/; 41/41 across computer-use/; 0 new typecheck errors; sacred SHA preserved; boot smoke confirms (displayTtlGc=null) fail-closed branch)

**Plan 248-03 (3 tasks — 3 commits: `e8a6ab01` test RED + `6ac2c3ac` feat GREEN + `0f9fcc95` feat wire-mcp-server)** — TTL Garbage Collector for idle nested displays, closing the v44 display-lifecycle loop opened by 248-01 (backend manager) + 248-02 (MCP tools). New `livinityd/source/modules/computer-use/displays/display-ttl-gc.ts` (120 lines) mirrors the Phase 246-05 pty-sessions/ttl-gc.ts factory pattern verbatim with displays-specific drift-locks: `DISPLAY_TTL_GC_DEFAULT_IDLE_MS = 14_400_000` (4h — DIFFERENT from PTY's 24h because nested X servers are heavier than PTYs and the shorter window bounds worst-case resource hold while still giving multi-hour UAT walks room) + `DISPLAY_TTL_GC_DEFAULT_SWEEP_MS = 3_600_000` (1h — SAME as PTY for operator-mental-model consistency) + factory `createDisplayTtlGc({displayManager, nowFn?, idleMs?, sweepMs?, setIntervalFn?, clearIntervalFn?, logger?}) → IdleDisplaySweep` whose `start()` is idempotent (second start clears prior handle), `stop()` is null-safe (repeatable), and `sweepNow()` returns Promise<number> of SUCCESSFUL kills. Algorithm: `cutoff = now() - idleMs`, await `displayManager.list()`, for each record where `Date.parse(last_app_at ?? created_at) < cutoff` call `displayManager.kill({display, callerSession: r.owner_session})` (D-248-03-A owner-impersonation lift — TTL GC reads owner_session off each record and passes it BACK into kill() as callerSession, bypassing the user-facing D-V44-DISPLAY-OWNER-SCOPED check in a well-scoped in-process code path that the test suite drift-locks; chosen over a `killAsSystem()` admin escape method because it requires zero new surface on DisplayManager and the existing kill() contract is sufficient); on `ok:true` increment killed counter + emit audit `logger.info('display-ttl-gc: killed idle display', {display, idleAgeMs, owner_session})`; on `ok:false` (most commonly 'not-found' when display vanished between list and kill) silently swallow + don't count; thrown exceptions also swallowed (best-effort). All time + timer functions DI'd — vitest never spins a real wall-clock interval. Additive `types.ts`: `DisplayRecord` gains optional `last_app_at?: string` (the field was already WRITTEN by `attachApp()` at 248-01 display-manager.ts:351 but list() didn't surface it through the typed return — D-248-03-C documents this as an in-scope additive change without retro-patching 248-01's SUMMARY drift-lock claims). Additive `display-manager.ts` list() spreads `last_app_at` into the returned record when present in the Redis hash (3-line change, zero test breakage in 248-01). Boot wiring in `mcp/server.ts`: `createDisplayTtlGc` import added next to `createDisplayManager`; after the existing displayManager construction block (when `displayManager !== undefined`) construct `displayTtlGc = createDisplayTtlGc({displayManager, logger:{info:(msg, ctx) => process.stderr.write('[luse-mcp] display-ttl-gc: ' + msg + (ctx ? ' ' + JSON.stringify(ctx) : '') + '\\n')}})`; null branch leaks no interval handle; `displayTtlGc.start()` invoked AFTER `await server.connect(transport)` (D-248-03-B — if connect throws, starting first would leak the 1h interval handle); `process.on('beforeExit', () => displayTtlGc.stop())` ensures vitest / dev restarts that exit gracefully don't leak the handle; final boot log line extended with `(displayTtlGc=${displayTtlGc !== undefined ? 'started' : 'null'})` so operators can confirm wiring from systemd journal at boot. New `__tests__/display-ttl-gc.test.ts` (286 lines, 8 vitest cases): Cases 1+2 drift-lock both constants exact literals (`4 * 60 * 60 * 1000` and `60 * 60 * 1000` AND `14_400_000` and `3_600_000`), Case 3 sweepNow with 1 stale (5h ago last_app_at) + 1 fresh (1h ago) → `kill` called ONCE with `{display:':10', callerSession:'s1'}` (owner-impersonation drift-lock) + count===1 + ':11' NOT touched, Case 4 no last_app_at → falls back to created_at (5h ago) → killed, Case 5 start() idempotent (second clears first handle via clearIntervalFn called with first setIntervalFn return value + both calls forward 5_000 sweepMs), Case 6 stop() null-safe (no throw before any start + repeatable after start), Case 7 best-effort kill (one `{ok:true}` + one `{ok:false, error:'not-found'}` → count===1 + 2 kill calls + no throw), Case 8 audit log shape (`logger.info('display-ttl-gc: killed idle display', {display:':10', idleAgeMs:18_000_000, owner_session:'s1'})` — exact field names + types pinned). Test fixtures use `makeDisplayManager(records: Partial<DisplayRecord>[])` helper returning `vi.fn()`-backed stubs for all 6 DisplayManager methods + `initialized: Promise.resolve()`; fixed epoch `FIXED_NOW = Date.parse('2026-05-28T12:00:00.000Z')` + `isoAgo(ms)` helper for round-trip-exact staleness math. **Sacred blob SHA `f3538e1d811992b782a9bb057d1b7f0a0189f95f`** of `liv/packages/core/src/sdk-agent-runner.ts` **PRESERVED** through all 3 commits (`[sacred-sha] PASS: 20 files verified` on every commit). **0 new typecheck errors** under `computer-use/displays/` or `computer-use/mcp/server.ts` (`pnpm tsc --noEmit | grep -E '(displays/|computer-use/mcp/server\\.ts)' | wc -l → 0`). **Boot smoke verified** — `LUSE_REDIS_URL='' pnpm tsx source/modules/computer-use/mcp/server.ts < /dev/null` emits `[luse-mcp] connected via stdio transport (redis=null, create_stream gated off) (displayManager=null) (displayTtlGc=null)` confirming the null-branch leaks no interval handle. Vitest: **8/8 GREEN** on display-ttl-gc.test.ts in 6ms; **23/23 GREEN** across the full displays/ folder (15 display-manager from 248-01 + 8 display-ttl-gc from 248-03); **41/41 GREEN** across displays/ + computer-use/mcp/tools.test.ts (adding the 18 from 248-02). Plan 248-04 (canonical agent-agnostic `docs/luse/DISPLAY-LIFECYCLE.md` + sync to 4 shim dirs) and 248-05 (Mini PC deploy + automated probes + UAT — operator greps `journalctl -u livinityd | grep '\\[luse-mcp\\] connected'` for `displayTtlGc=started` as the single-line probe) now unblocked. v44.0 milestone progress: **2/4 phases artifact-complete + 3/5 plans of phase 248 shipped** (P246 + P247 done; P248 3/5 plans done; P249 close still planned). Pattern names verified visible in Aion shim: `Pattern 1` × 2, `screenshot-then-act` × 4, `landmark-anchored` × 6, `Focus-before-type` × 1. **D-247-A (Gemini)**: honored Phase 242 D-242-C verbatim — Gemini gets a section in `INTEGRATION-RECIPES.md` documenting MCP tool-discovery as the ONLY access path; no `.gemini/skills/luse.md` shim created. **Sacred SHA `f3538e1d811992b782a9bb057d1b7f0a0189f95f` of `liv/packages/core/src/sdk-agent-runner.ts` PRESERVED through all 3 implementation commits** (pre-commit hook `[sacred-sha] PASS: 20 files verified` on every commit). New `livinityd/source/modules/pty-sessions/ttl-gc.ts` (90 lines) exports drift-locked constants `TTL_GC_DEFAULT_IDLE_MS = 86_400_000` (24h) + `TTL_GC_DEFAULT_SWEEP_MS = 3_600_000` (1h) + factory `createTtlGc({sessionManager, nowFn?, idleMs?, sweepMs?, setIntervalFn?, clearIntervalFn?, logger?}) → IdleSweep` whose `start()` is idempotent (replaces prior interval handle), `stop()` is null-handle safe (repeatable), and `sweepNow()` returns the kill count for a synchronous one-shot scan. Algorithm: `cutoff = now() - idleMs`, iterate `sessionManager.entries()`, for each session whose `Date.parse(lastAttachAt) < cutoff` call `sessionManager.kill(id)` + audit-log `ttl-gc: killed idle session {id, idleAgeMs}`. ## Previous Position (v43 — Phase 238.9 ✅ SHIPPED — Adaptive donut ACTUALLY works (2-file CSS @media switch); CSS bg-image sandbox bug FIXED, cumulative 10 hot-fix phases (238→238.9))

**Plan 238.9-01 (2-task — `d13bd1df` feat split SVGs + `7842706a` fix install-script branding asset list)** — Operator 2026-05-27 night: "Aydinlik temada halka siyah icindeki nokta beyaz olmali! Karanlik temada halka beyaz icindeki nokta siyah olmaliydi" — confirming Phase 238.8's adaptive donut wasn't actually adapting. **Root cause discovery:** SVG internal `<style>` `@media (prefers-color-scheme)` rules are SANDBOXED OFF when SVG is referenced via CSS `background-image: url(...)` — browsers load CSS-bg SVGs in a restricted context that disables @media queries. Works ONLY for `<img>`, `<object>`, `<link rel="icon">`. **Fix:** ship TWO separate SVG files + use CSS @media query on the host page (which DOES work regardless of how the SVG is referenced). (1) `caddy/branding/favicon-light.svg` (NEW, 459B): outer `#0a0a0a` black ring + inner `#ffffff` white dot. (2) `caddy/branding/favicon-dark.svg` (NEW, 446B): outer `#f5f5f7` white ring + inner `#050507` black dot. (3) `caddy/branding/livinity-overlay.css`: `.liv-brand-donut` defaults to favicon-light; `@media (prefers-color-scheme: dark) .liv-brand-donut` switches to favicon-dark; ALSO `[data-theme='dark'] .liv-brand-donut` override so AionUi's in-app Arco theme picker triggers the flip without needing OS-level preference change. (4) `scripts/install-liv-assistant.sh` Phase 232 branding-copy loop extended from 3 → 5 assets (added favicon-light.svg + favicon-dark.svg). **Mini PC deploy:** both SVGs copied (446B/459B), CSS served 6933B with 3 light-refs + 4 dark-refs + 6 prefers-color-scheme + 1 data-theme rules. External GET 200 for both /liv/branding/favicon-{light,dark}.svg. Phase 238.8 marker (liv-brand-donut) in JS bundle = 1 (reused, not regenerated). Phase 238/238.1/238.2/238.4/238.5/238.6/238.7/238.8 all non-regressed. LICENSE+NOTICE byte-identical. Sacred SHA `f3538e1d...` UNCHANGED. 6/6 services active. **Operator action:** browser hard-reload `https://bruce.livinity.io/liv/` → sidebar donut adapts correctly: light theme = black ring + white dot, dark theme = white ring + black dot. Identical to livinity.io website tab favicon behavior. AionUi's own Arco theme picker (Settings → Theme) also flips the donut. **Cumulative v43 visible-rebrand: 10 hot-fix phases (238→238.9) FULLY COMPLETE — adaptive donut now works correctly + every visible Aion surface replaced.**

## Previous Position (v42 — Phase 237 ✅ SHIPPED 1/1 plan — AionUi WS handshake RFC 6455 compliance fix LIVE on Mini PC, chat streaming WORKS without page reload, 12/12 SCs GREEN, sacred SHA UNCHANGED)

**Plan 237-01 (3-task hot-fix — `f6c784b7` feat caddy split + `ec2588c4` docs PLAN + deploy log + docs commit this SUMMARY)** — URGENT hot-fix continuation of Phase 236 in response to operator live-browser test post-236 deploy. Operator confirmed icons load + app opens faster, but `wss://bruce.livinity.io/ws` STILL FAILING — chat streaming broken (operator: "ben chat e yazi yaziyorum ama yazi hemen gelmiyor ben chatden ayrildiktan sonra yazi geliyor"). **Root cause:** Per RFC 6455 § 4.1 browsers do NOT send a `Referer` header on the WS upgrade handshake — only `Origin`. Phase 236's combined `@liv_subresource` matcher (`header_regexp Referer ... AND path /api/* /ws /ws/*`) silently MISSED the WS upgrade → fell through to `:8080` catch-all (no `/ws` route on livinityd) → 404/502 → chat unusable until manual reload. Phase 236 EXT-4 falsely passed because curl explicitly set `-H "Referer: .../liv/"`; the real browser is FORCED into the negative-control case (Phase 236 EXT-4b "WITHOUT Referer → 502" was the canary). **Fix (Task 1):** Split `LIV_ASSISTANT_SUBRESOURCE_HANDLE` constant in `livos/packages/livinityd/source/modules/domain/caddy.ts` into two matchers (constant name preserved so 3 emit sites untouched): `@liv_ws path /ws /ws/*` UNCONDITIONAL (no header check — AionUi exclusively owns `/ws` on this host; livinityd has no `/ws` route → safe + consistent) + `@liv_api_subresource { header_regexp Referer ^https?://[^/]+/liv(/|$); path /api/* }` (KEEP referer-gate for `/api/*` only — preserves Phase 236's LivOS-shell `/api/*` collateral protection). Both proxy to `127.0.0.1:3020` with identical header-strip + `WS_TRANSPORT_BODY`. `caddy.test.ts` Phase 236 describe REPLACED with 13 new Phase 237 assertions; 72 → 74 PASS. UI build clean (33.46s). Typecheck: 2 pre-existing baseline errors confirmed (Phase 236 baseline) — zero new errors. **Mini PC deploy (Task 2):** single batched SSH session ran `update.sh` EXIT 0 + 11-step verify. Deployed SHA `f6c784b7b7f1c71c99a582d567150d0e029915d1`. Caddyfile delta proven: `@liv_subresource` 2 → 0 (old GONE), `@liv_ws` 0 → 2, `@liv_api_subresource` 0 → 2, `@liv path` 1 → 1 (Phase 226-04 preserved). 6/6 services active. Sacred SHA Mini PC sha256 `62f924594e81331afb159a9a50ef718ef3eb7e79cd5287d9bd2e4788cbab1bfe` MATCH (Phase 235/236 snapshot). **External smoke (Cloudflare→Server5→Mini PC tunnel):** EXT-1 WS upgrade **WITHOUT Referer** (Origin only, browser-realistic) → **HTTP 101 Switching Protocols + valid `Sec-Websocket-Accept`** (was 502 under Phase 236) — THE FIX PROOF; EXT-2 `/api/assets/logos/ai-major/claude.svg` (Referer=/liv/) → 200 + svg + 1697 bytes (Phase 236 non-regressed); EXT-3 `/` (LivOS shell) → 200 + text/html; EXT-4 `/liv/api/auth/status` → 200 + JSON (Phase 235 non-regressed). Local STEP 7 `/api/* WITHOUT Referer` → 404 from livinityd catch-all (proves @liv_api_subresource collateral guard preserved). STEP 8 `/liv-login` → 302 (Phase 234 non-regressed). **All 12 SCs PASS. Zero deviations.** Pre-commit `[sacred-sha] PASS: 20 files verified` fired on both Task 1 (`f6c784b7`) + Task 2 (`ec2588c4`) commits. SSH discipline: 1 batched session (well within fail2ban tolerance). DEPLOY-LOG at `.planning/phases/237-aionui-ws-handshake-fix/237-DEPLOY-LOG.md`. SUMMARY at `237-SUMMARY.md`. PLAN at `237-PLAN.md`. **Operator action:** browser Ctrl+F5 on `https://bruce.livinity.io/` — chat will now stream responses in real-time.

## Previous Position (v42 — Phase 234 ✅ SHIPPED 4/4 plans — Liv AI polish COMPLETE, all 8 SCs GREEN, sacred SHA UNCHANGED, Mini PC LIVE)

**Plan 234-04 (auth bypass — Option B Modified, atomic feat commit `b64edccd` + fix commit `49284b92` + docs commit)** — Wave 4 livinityd auth-bypass SHIPPED on Mini PC. New `livos/packages/livinityd/source/modules/server/liv-login-handler.ts` (`makeLivLoginHandler(redis)` Express factory) mints an AionUi qr-token via loopback `POST http://127.0.0.1:3020/api/webui/generate-qr-token`, exchanges it for an `aionui-session` JWT via `POST /api/auth/qr-login {qr_token}`, captures the upstream `Set-Cookie: aionui-session=...; Path=/; HttpOnly; SameSite=Lax; Max-Age=2592000`, forwards it to the browser response unchanged, then 302-redirects to `/liv/`. The browser stores the cookie scoped to bruce.livinity.io and the iframe's subsequent `/liv/*` requests auto-include it — the AionUi SPA sees `is_authenticated:true` and renders the chat surface directly with no login form. Companion vitest `liv-login-handler.test.ts` uses a `node:http.createServer` mock of the AionUi loopback endpoints (port-rewriting via patched `globalThis.fetch`) to cover 6 cases: flag missing (enabled), flag='true' (enabled), flag='false' (disabled, no qr-token fetch), flag='TRUE' (non-'false' = enabled), qr-token mint failure (safety hatch 302 to /liv/ no Set-Cookie), no Set-Cookie response (safety hatch). **6/6 vitest GREEN** (428ms). **Wire-up:** `livos/packages/livinityd/source/index.ts` imports the factory and wires `this.server.app.get('/liv-login', makeLivLoginHandler(this.ai.redis))` immediately after the `NativeAppConfigStore` block (after `this.ai.start()` finishes so Redis is live). Try/catch guards a missing `this.server.app`. **Iframe src swap:** `livos/packages/ui/src/modules/window/app-contents/liv-assistant-window.tsx` `LIV_ASSISTANT_DEFAULT_URL` flipped from `'/liv/'` to `'/liv-login'`. `VITE_LIV_ASSISTANT_URL` env override semantic preserved. **Auto-fix during execution (Rule 1+3):** first Mini PC live probe returned HTTP 200 + Content-Type: text/html for `/liv-login` — the path-aware SPA fallback at `server/index.ts:1857` matched the catch-all `*` wildcard before Express dispatched to my late-mounted handler. Identical root cause to Phase 207 R5 for `/openclawos/*`: routes mounted in `livinityd.start()` AFTER `server.start()` are shadowed by the catch-all. Fix added `'/liv-login'` entry to `apiPathPrefixes` so the fallback `next()`s through. Shipped as separate `fix(234-04)` commit `49284b92` (NEVER amend rule). **Mini PC deploy:** `git push origin master` advanced `67587da1..b64edccd..49284b92`; single batched SSH (fail2ban discipline) ran `sudo bash /opt/livos/update.sh` twice — once for feat commit, once for fix commit (each EXIT 0 with `Deployed SHA recorded: b64edcc` then `49284b9`). Redis URL-form auth: `redis-cli -u "redis://default:<pw>@127.0.0.1:6379" SET liv:config:liv_ai_autologin_enabled true → OK`. **External SC-06 PASS (canonical):** `curl -i https://bruce.livinity.io/liv-login` returns `HTTP/1.1 302 Found` + `Set-Cookie: aionui-session=eyJ0eXAiOiJKV1QiLCJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiJ9.<JWT>; Path=/; HttpOnly; SameSite=Lax; Max-Age=2592000` + `location: /liv/` + `via: 1.1 Caddy`. Cookie-jar follow-up: `curl -b /tmp/jar -c /tmp/jar /liv-login → 302` then `curl -b /tmp/jar /liv/api/auth/status → {"success":true,"needs_setup":false,"user_count":1,"is_authenticated":true}` — operator IS authenticated through the entire CDN+Caddy+livinityd+AionUi chain. **External SC-08 PASS (reversibility):** `SET liv:config:liv_ai_autologin_enabled false → /liv-login returns 302+Location:/liv/ WITHOUT Set-Cookie` (safety hatch). `SET ... true → Set-Cookie restored`. Default semantic (missing OR non-`false` = enabled) covered by vitest Tests 1+4. **External SC-07 PASS (sacred SHA):** `git hash-object liv/packages/core/src/sdk-agent-runner.ts` = `f3538e1d811992b782a9bb057d1b7f0a0189f95f` UNCHANGED. Pre-commit hook `[sacred-sha] PASS: 20 files verified` on both commits. **Phase 233 UAT subset 5/5 GREEN (no regression):** `/liv/` HTML body has 3 'Liv AI' hits + 0 'AionUi' hits + `<title>Liv AI</title>`; `/liv/api/auth/status` reachable HTTP 200 returning standard JSON. **Phase 234 closes 4/4 plans + 8/8 SCs GREEN.** **Liv AI polish COMPLETE:** bigger window (1280x800 from Plan 02), chat-style dock icon (Plan 02), 'Liv AI' brand string throughout (wrapper Plan 02 + vendored binary Plan 03), auto-login removes the AionUi login form (Plan 04), all gated by Redis feature flag (`liv:config:liv_ai_autologin_enabled`) for reversibility. ## Previous Position (v42 — Phase 234 🟡 IN PROGRESS 1/4 plans — Plan 01 investigation + spec-lock SHIPPED, Plan 04 auth-bypass strategy LOCKED to Option B modified, sacred SHA UNCHANGED)

**Plan 234-01 (investigation + ADR spec-lock)** — Single-task evidence-gathering pass producing `.planning/phases/234-liv-ai-polish-ux/234-01-INVESTIGATION.md` (~890 lines, 9 sections A-I + Plan-spec lock). ZERO source-code edits; pure read+grep+curl+ssh+document. 4 batched Mini PC SSH sessions over ~30min (within fail2ban tolerance per `feedback_ssh_rate_limit`). **Auth surface evidence (Section A):** AionUi 2.1.4 exposes NO password-login endpoint (404 on /api/login, /api/auth/login, /api/auth/signin). The only working login path is `POST /api/webui/generate-qr-token` → `POST /api/auth/qr-login {qr_token}` → server returns `Set-Cookie: aionui-session=<JWT>; Path=/; HttpOnly; SameSite=Lax; Max-Age=2592000` (JWT iss=aionui aud=aionui-webui exp=iat+86400). Tested end-to-end through Caddy at `https://bruce.livinity.io/liv/` — full flow returns `is_authenticated:true` on subsequent `/liv/api/auth/status` calls. **Config knob search (Section A.9):** zero auth-disable flag in JS bundle, env, config files, or systemd unit. Option A DEAD. **Sandbox cookie injection (Section C.4):** AionUi session cookie is HttpOnly; JavaScript CANNOT set HttpOnly cookies (MDN spec). Original Plan 04 Option B sketch (iframe contentDocument cookie injection) is mechanically impossible. **Caddy LIV_ASSISTANT_HANDLE (Section C.2):** `/liv/*` reverse-proxied to `127.0.0.1:3020` on same eTLD+1 as LivOS shell; CSP `frame-ancestors 'self' https://bruce.livinity.io` permits iframe mount. **ADR Selected Option (Section H):** Option B (modified) — new livinityd Express handler at `GET /liv-login` performs qr-mint + qr-login server-side via loopback, forwards `Set-Cookie` to browser unchanged, 302-redirects to `/liv/`. Browser stores HttpOnly cookie scoped to `bruce.livinity.io` (Path=/), iframe's subsequent `/liv/*` requests auto-include it, SPA renders chat surface directly — no login form ever appears. Redis flag `liv:config:liv_ai_autologin_enabled` (default ON) gates the bypass. No Caddyfile changes needed (`/liv-login` falls through to default `handle { reverse_proxy 127.0.0.1:8080 }` already serving livinityd). **Naming collision (Section G):** G.1 preferred — rename LIVINITY_liv-assistant.name to 'Liv AI' AND delete the orphan LIVINITY_liv-ai entry (Phase 231 retirement cleanup). 8 cascading edits locked in Plan 02 spec. **Brand-string inventory (Section F):** ~33 files REPLACE under `/opt/liv-assistant/current/static/` (2 html + 30 js + 1 css); LICENSE/NOTICE/UPSTREAM.md/package.json/binaries all EXCLUDE; repo-side mostly KEEP (comments + variable names + provenance docs intentional). Plan 03 sed pattern locked: `s/AionUi/Liv AI/g; s/aionui-web/liv-ai-web/g; s/aionui/liv-ai/g` with pre-check idempotency guard. **Two minor deviations (Rule 3 auto-fix):** (1) credentials path corrected from spec — actual file is `/etc/livos/liv-assistant-credentials` per `scripts/capture-liv-assistant-password.sh` (Phase 223-03 ground truth), not `/etc/liv-assistant/credentials` as plan frontmatter sketched; (2) `POST /api/webui/reset-password` probe accidentally rotated the admin password (returns new pw) — benign because Plan 04 chose Option B (qr-login, NOT password-login) so the credentials file is unused by the auth-bypass path. Sacred SHA `f3538e1d811992b782a9bb057d1b7f0a0189f95f` UNCHANGED on commit (pre-commit hook gates). 9 PLAN verify gates all PASS (file exists, 9 H2 sections, 3 Plan-spec sub-sections, `### Selected option:` block, `## Plan-spec lock` H2, sacred SHA match). **Plan 234-01 closes 1/1 → Phase 234 advances: 234-01 ✅ + 234-02 ⚪ READY + 234-03 ⚪ READY + 234-04 ⚪ STRATEGY-LOCKED (orchestrator must rewrite PLAN.md `<action>` block from Section I before Plan 04 executes).** Next: Plan 234-02 (UI polish — window 1280x800 + chat icon + 'Liv AI' brand + LIVINITY_liv-ai cleanup). Resume: `/gsd:execute-phase 234 --auto`. See [234-01-INVESTIGATION.md](phases/234-liv-ai-polish-ux/234-01-INVESTIGATION.md) + [234-01-SUMMARY.md](phases/234-liv-ai-polish-ux/234-01-SUMMARY.md).

## Previous Position (v42 — Phase 227 🟡 1/3 plans — LivAssistantWindow component + 4 jsdom unit tests GREEN, sacred SHA UNCHANGED)

**Plan 227-01 (`49a08391`)** — New `LivAssistantWindow` React component (default export) at `livos/packages/ui/src/modules/window/app-contents/liv-assistant-window.tsx` (65 lines) mirroring the existing `liv-ai-chat-iframe-content.tsx` same-origin iframe pattern (`h-full w-full border-0 bg-background`). iframe `src` defaults to the RELATIVE path `'/liv/'` (Phase 226-04 Caddy handle) via top-level const `LIV_ASSISTANT_DEFAULT_URL` so the component is host-agnostic across apex (`bruce.livinity.io`), multi-user subdomains, and the null-mainDomain dev fallback; env override via `import.meta.env.VITE_LIV_ASSISTANT_URL` for staging exceptions. iframe carries the test-locked sandbox token list `'allow-same-origin allow-scripts allow-forms allow-popups allow-downloads'` (exposed as named const `LIV_ASSISTANT_SANDBOX` — friction to loosen because future edits must touch both the constant and the matching exact-equality assertion in the test). `title='Liv Assistant'`, `data-testid='liv-assistant-iframe'`, and `allow='clipboard-read; clipboard-write'` round out the contract. New jsdom unit test at `livos/packages/ui/src/modules/window/app-contents/liv-assistant-window.unit.test.tsx` (94 lines) uses **direct react-dom/client + createRoot harness — NO `@testing-library/react`** (D-NO-NEW-DEPS, mirrors Phase 224-03 `v42-migration-banner.test.tsx` byte-for-byte) with 4 assertions: (1) exactly one iframe rendered, src ends `/liv/`, title equals `'Liv Assistant'`, `LIV_ASSISTANT_DEFAULT_URL === '/liv/'`; (2) `sandbox` attribute equals the exact literal `'allow-same-origin allow-scripts allow-forms allow-popups allow-downloads'`; (3) `allow` contains both `'clipboard-read'` AND `'clipboard-write'`; (4) `className` contains both `'h-full'` AND `'w-full'`. ## Previous Position (v42 — Phase 226 ❌ BLOCKED 2/3 plans + Rule 4 architectural — caddy.ts dynamic generator owns Caddyfile) [RESOLVED by Plan 226-04]

**Plan 226-03 (commit `1e56b8c9`)** — Mini PC deploy HALTED at Step 2 preflight per the plan's own `BLOCKED:` gate. Rule 4 architectural STOP discovered: `/etc/caddy/Caddyfile` on Mini PC is dynamically generated by `livos/packages/livinityd/source/modules/domain/caddy.ts` (Phase 86 / Phase 218 lineage) — NOT statically managed. **Finding 1 (Rule 1 bug):** Plan 226-01's installer regex `^[[:space:]]*bruce\.livinity\.io[[:space:]]*\{` misses the live `http://bruce.livinity.io {` form (tunnel-mode scheme prefix from caddy.ts line 342); installer would hit hard-fail branch. **Finding 2 (Rule 4 architectural):** caddy.ts line 47 (`CADDYFILE_PATH = '/etc/caddy/Caddyfile'`), line 590 (`writeFile(...)`), 3 `reloadCaddy()` call sites — livinityd rewrites the file from scratch on every regen (app install / share / subdomain change); any in-place installer edits would be wiped. **Finding 3:** Plan 226-01's strategy (drop snippet under `/etc/caddy/conf.d/` + add 2 `import` lines to `/etc/caddy/Caddyfile`) is structurally incompatible — the conf.d drop survives but the import lines don't. **No live changes made:** Mini PC `/opt/livos/update.sh` still pre-Phase-226 (`c3ba5f52...`), no conf.d/ created, all 6 services unchanged. Sacred SHA `f3538e1d811992b782a9bb057d1b7f0a0189f95f` byte-identical Mini PC vs repo (Mini PC `sha256sum` = `62f924594e8133...` matches local `sha256sum`; local `git hash-object` = canonical SHA). SC verdicts: **SC-05 PASS** (sacred SHA unchanged), **SC-06 PASS** (Caddyfile already `bruce:bruce 644` pre-deploy — Phase 218 lineage), **SC-01/02/03/04 NOT-EXERCISED** (preflight halt — installer never invoked, no /liv handler in Caddy config). **CRITICAL SIDE-EFFECT:** commits `bef03544` (update.sh Step 4.7) + `1e56b8c9` are on master + pushed to GitHub — **next `bash /opt/livos/update.sh` on Mini PC will self-rsync new content AND hit installer fail at Step 4.7, aborting deploy** (services NOT touched because Step 4.7 precedes Step 8 restarts, but update.sh becomes unrunnable until reconciliation). Three architectural options for closure (operator selection required): **Option A (RECOMMENDED) — Phase 226-04 patches caddy.ts to emit `/liv` reverse_proxy handler inline inside bruce.livinity.io block emitter** (~50 LOC + 1 test case + remove/no-op update.sh Step 4.7); **Option B — inline /liv handler + drop snippet file entirely**; **Option C — hybrid: caddy.ts emits top-level `import conf.d/*.caddy` + in-block `import liv_assistant` (both regen-survivable) + installer still lays the snippet file**. **Key decisions (Phase 195-04):**

- Native `URL().hostname` strict-equality allow-list (NOT regex substring match) — defeats subdomain trick (`https://x.ai.evil.example.com/`) and userinfo trick (`https://x.ai@evil.example.com/`) that a naive regex would let through.
- `react-dom/client` harness + `vi.mock('@/trpc/trpc')` instead of `@testing-library/react` — D-NO-NEW-DEPS preserved. Canonical pattern from `inline-tool-pill.unit.test.tsx`.
- Pure helpers `isXaiOAuthUrl` + `mapScopesToDisplay` exported from component — tests lock security + scope contracts independent of React render lifecycle (bonus tests #6 + #7).
- Continue button gated by `state.kind !== 'connected'` — Continue only enables after real successful auth. Wizard navigation contract (`{onContinue, onSkip, onBack}`) preserved verbatim.
- Speech / transcription chips never surfaced (T-195-04-04 spoofing defense extended to UI strings) — mapScopesToDisplay returns ['Tools','Image','Video'] for api:access scope but never includes any chip for speech / transcription. Acceptance criterion `grep -cE "Voice|audio" connect-ai-step.tsx` = 0 enforced.

- as unknown as Array<...> double cast for computeGraphStats TypeScript overlap between GraphNode shapes

## 160-05 Status (2026-05-19) — computer_read_file Path Sandbox CODE-COMPLETE (2 commits, sacred SHA preserved 2/2, D-09 verbatim invariant preserved)

- **COMPUTER_READ_FILE PATH SANDBOX** (Plan 5 of 6 in Phase 160, 2 tasks): closes the LLM-controlled file-read jailbreak vector flagged in the Phase 159 review. Adds a per-user allowlist (`/home/<user>/` + `/tmp/luse-*/` + `/opt/livos/data/uploads/<userId>/`) gated by `fs.realpath` symlink resolution BEFORE the allowlist check (so an allowlisted symlink targeting `/etc/passwd` is still rejected). NUL-byte pre-flight reject blocks null-byte truncation attacks. Rejection error echoes `requested=… resolved=… (allowed prefixes: …)` for agent self-correction but never leaks file content. `LUSE_USER_ID` env (set by `luse-mcp-config` when the child is spawned) drives both userSlug + userId allowlist branches; defaults to `'bruce'` for host-display single-user case (matches live Mini PC user per MEMORY). Two atomic commits:
  1. `5def1871` Task 1 — `livos/packages/livinityd/source/modules/computer-use/mcp/tools.ts` (+139/-17 — `realpath as nodeRealpath` import added next to existing `readdir as nodeReaddir`; `__setRealpathForTest` test seam exported mirroring `__setReaddirForTest` pattern; `isPathAllowed(resolved, userSlug, userId)` exported pure helper; `computer_read_file` handler rewritten with NUL-reject → realpath → allowlist guard while preserving the original `readFileBase64` + base64-wrap return shape beneath the guard) + `livos/packages/livinityd/source/modules/computer-use/mcp/tools.test.ts` (T12 updated to use allowlisted `/home/bruce/foo.txt` path with `__setRealpathForTest(async (p) => String(p))` stub so the original assertion still meaningfully exercises the post-guard read path).
  2. `42b04ff2` Task 2 — `livos/packages/livinityd/source/modules/computer-use/mcp/tools.test.ts` (+213 — two new describe blocks: "Phase 160-05 — computer_read_file sandbox" with 7 source-text invariants locking the sandbox literal (`isPathAllowed` helper, all 3 allowlist branches, realpath-BEFORE-allowlist source-order assertion, rejection-error shape with resolved-path leak + no content leak, `LUSE_USER_ID` env wire); "Phase 160-05 — computer_read_file sandbox runtime rejection" with 8 runtime tests driving hostile inputs via the realpath stub: `/etc/passwd` absolute reject, `../../etc/shadow` traversal reject (post-realpath collapse), NUL-byte path reject (asserts realpath spy NEVER called), symlink-out-of-jail reject (allowed entry path + evil resolved target), accept cases for `/tmp/luse-*/` + `/opt/livos/data/uploads/bruce/`, empty-path reject, and ENOENT realpath-error path).
- **Verification:** `mcp/tools.test.ts` **65 PASS / 0 FAIL** (was 50/0 — added 7 Phase 160-05 source-text invariants + 8 runtime rejection/accept cases = +15 total). Each runtime rejection test asserts `readFileBase64` was NEVER called — proves the guard fires before the disk read.
- **Sacred SHA `f3538e1d811992b782a9bb057d1b7f0a0189f95f`** for `liv/packages/core/src/sdk-agent-runner.ts` PRESERVED across both source commits.
- **D-09 verbatim contract upheld:** `git rev-parse HEAD:livos/packages/livinityd/source/modules/computer-use/luse-system-prompt.ts` = `2083f0a3dfc798b4841613b9576b94929f2faf2f` — unchanged from baseline.
- **D-NO-NEW-DEPS upheld:** `git diff --stat HEAD~2..HEAD -- **/package.json` = empty.
- **Files-modified disjoint from Plan 160-04:** my 2 files are `mcp/tools.ts` + `mcp/tools.test.ts`; Plan 160-04 touches `agent-prompt-builder.ts` + `screenshot.ts` (+ untracked `display-size.ts`). Zero overlap — parallel safety contract honored.
- **Deviations (3 auto-fixed; documented in 160-05-SUMMARY.md):**
  1. **Rule 2 — Security:** Added NUL-byte pre-flight reject BEFORE realpath. Plan snippet had no NUL handling; executor prompt's `success_criteria` explicitly required it. Runtime test asserts the reject fires + realpath spy NEVER called.
  2. **Rule 3 — Blocking:** T12 (`computer_read_file` original test) used `/tmp/foo.txt` which the new sandbox correctly rejects → fixed by updating T12 to use `/home/bruce/foo.txt` with `__setRealpathForTest` stub so the original `readFileBase64 was called with the right path + result wrapped as MCP content` intent is preserved.
  3. **Rule 1 — Cosmetic:** Used `'bruce'` default for `LUSE_USER_ID` fallback instead of plan's `'admin'`. Matches live Mini PC single-tenant user (per MEMORY: `bruce@10.69.31.68` + `/home/bruce/`). Defaulting to `admin` would mean host-display reads fail against nonexistent `/home/admin/`.
- **Beyond-plan additions (security):** Plan called for 7 source-text invariants; I added 8 RUNTIME tests on top because executor `success_criteria` explicitly required them: "must reject /etc/passwd, ../../etc/shadow, NUL-byte path, symlink-out-of-jail". Source-text invariants catch literal-string drift; runtime tests catch logic drift (e.g. accidentally inverting the allowlist check). Together they double-cover the security policy.
- **Deferred (out of scope per scope-boundary rule):** Same pre-existing failures in `luse-mcp-config.test.ts` T4/T5/T6 and other native/* tests already documented in 160-02 + 160-03 summaries. None touched by this plan.

## 104-13 Status (2026-05-12) — pnpm blockExoticSubdeps hotfix SHIPPED (71/71 host-side bash test PASS; unblocks baileys → libsignal git-repository subdep on pnpm 11+)

- **HOTFIX** (13th plan added to phase after 104-12): 104-13 fixes the second mainserver 154.53.56.75 re-deploy failure mode on 104-12's `deploy-livinityd.sh`. Modern Ubuntu 24.04 hosts get pnpm 11.1.1+ via `npm install -g pnpm@latest`, which enforces `blockExoticSubdeps` by default. pnpm refuses to install `libsignal` (a legitimate `baileys@6.7.21` WhatsApp subdep that comes from a `git-repository` URL) and `pnpm install` fails with `[ERR_PNPM_EXOTIC_SUBDEP]`. Mini PC unaffected — older pnpm there doesn't enforce the gate. Two commits:
  1. `85b0b3ef` `scripts/install/deploy-livinityd.sh` (1 new helper + pipeline wire): `_dld_write_pnpm_npmrc` writes/appends `block-exotic-subdeps=false` to `${_DLD_LIVOS_DIR}/.npmrc` (i.e. `/opt/livos/.npmrc`). Idempotent: greps for existing `^block-exotic-subdeps=` line and short-circuits with `ok` log when present; only appends when absent. Wired into `deploy_livinityd` AFTER `_dld_clone_source` (so `/opt/livos/` exists) and BEFORE `_dld_build_packages` (so pnpm sees the file at install time). Header comment block documents the supply-chain tradeoff (relaxes the gate for ALL git-resolved subdeps, not just `libsignal`) and lists the deferred audit checklist (review every git-resolved subdep, pin baileys to libsignal-free version, switch to npm-published libsignal-client wrapper). Pipeline header comment + final `ok` line updated to mention 104-13. Plus `.planning/phases/104-local-install-and-docker-uat/104-13-PLAN.md`.
  2. `(this commit)` `scripts/install/__tests__/test-deploy-livinityd.sh` (TEST 15 added, 66 → 71 assertions: (a) `_dld_write_pnpm_npmrc()` function defined, (b) literal `block-exotic-subdeps=false` present in source, (c) helper targets `.npmrc` under `_DLD_LIVOS_DIR`, (d) idempotent `grep -q "^block-exotic-subdeps="` guard present, (e) call-order check using awk-extracted `deploy_livinityd` body: clone < npmrc-write < build). Plus 104-13-SUMMARY.md + STATE.md + ROADMAP.md updates.
- Test results: `bash scripts/install/__tests__/test-deploy-livinityd.sh` → 71 PASS, 0 FAIL. Plus 104-08 test-mode-hybrid-args.sh 18/18 still PASS + 104-09 test-mode-tunnel-args.sh 24/24 still PASS. **Combined: 18 + 24 + 71 = 113 PASS across 3 test files (up from 108 after 104-12).**
- Sacred SHA `f3538e1d811992b782a9bb057d1b7f0a0189f95f` UNTOUCHED across both commits (verified via `git hash-object liv/packages/core/src/sdk-agent-runner.ts` post each commit).
- D-104-NO-PROD-IMPACT preserved: mode-cloud.sh untouched (TEST 6 negative-grep still PASS); 104-08/104-09 regression smoke still PASS.
- D-104-RELAY-ZERO-DATA-PLANE preserved: deploy-livinityd.sh has ZERO Server5 / livinity.io / nexus.livinity / relay.livinity references — only network calls are git clone (GitHub), apt-get, optional 104-10 heartbeat.
- D-104-13-SECURITY-TRADEOFF: setting `block-exotic-subdeps=false` relaxes pnpm's supply-chain safety for ALL git-resolved subdeps, not just `libsignal`. Accepted because in our current `pnpm-lock.yaml` the only git-resolved subdep IS the known-good upstream `libsignal`. Deferred audit checklist documented in helper source comment + 104-13-SUMMARY.md (a) review every git-resolved subdep, (b) pin baileys to libsignal-free version when available, (c) switch to npm-published libsignal-client wrapper so the gate can be re-enabled.
- D-104-13-IDEMPOTENT-APPEND: helper greps for existing directive before appending — no double-write on re-run. Critical because install.sh is supposed to be re-runnable.
- Deviations: NONE (no Rule 1/2/3 fixes needed; the plan was specific about file path, directive literal, idempotency pattern, and call order).
- Carry-forward: **mainserver 154.53.56.75 re-deploy remains the orchestrator's NEXT step.** Re-run `bash install.sh --mode hybrid --domain test.livinity.live --cf-token X --cf-zone-id Y` on mainserver. Confirm: pnpm install succeeds (no more `[ERR_PNPM_EXOTIC_SUBDEP]`); all 4 systemd services active (`systemctl is-active livos liv-core liv-worker liv-memory` returns 4× `active`); `https://test.livinity.live` shows the LivOS login screen. THIS IS THE GO/NO-GO GATE FOR CLOSING PHASE 104.

## 104-12 Status (2026-05-12) — deploy-livinityd path-bug fix + liv-stack SHIPPED (66/66 host-side bash test PASS; closes 104-11's nested-path ENOENT failure + adds liv-core/liv-worker/liv-memory systemd units)

- **HOTFIX + SCOPE-EXTENSION** (12th plan added to phase after 104-11): 104-12 fixes the critical nested-`/opt/livos/livos/` rsync bug in 104-11's `deploy-livinityd.sh` that caused `pnpm install` to fail with `ENOENT: no such file or directory, scandir '/opt/livos/liv/packages/core'` on the mainserver 154.53.56.75 live test today, AND extends the deploy to also produce liv-core/liv-worker/liv-memory systemd units (closing the scope boundary 104-11 carried forward as "deferred to 104-12"). Root cause of the bug: livinityd's package.json declares `"@liv/core": "file:../../../liv/packages/core"` — that relative path resolves from `/opt/livos/packages/livinityd/`, three levels up = `/opt/liv/packages/core`. 104-11's rsync produced `/opt/livos/livos/packages/livinityd/` (nested) so `../../../liv` = `/liv` (does not exist). Three commits:
  1. `7a708430` `scripts/install/deploy-livinityd.sh` (299 insertions, 40 deletions). If .env is absent but PG role exists, defensive `ALTER USER livos WITH PASSWORD` aligns cluster with generated password. Rationale: operators running install.sh twice (e.g. to pick up a Caddy fix) shouldn't lose database access.
- D-104-11-HEALTH-NONFATAL: health check failure (livinityd not bound to :8080 within 30s) WARNs and continues — does NOT exit non-zero. Rationale: at health-check time, .env + systemd unit + Caddy reload are already done; failing now leaves the operator in a half-installed state with no easy recovery. Far better to print loud `journalctl -u livos.service -n 50` guidance and let operator debug from known-good install marker.

## 104-10 Status (2026-05-12) — LivOS heartbeat client SHIPPED (43/43 vitest PASS in 3.34s; first client-side piece of v34)

- **v34 SEED** (10th plan added to phase after 104-09 hotfix): 104-10 ships the LivOS → livinity.io heartbeat client — the FIRST client-side piece of v34 (LivOS ↔ livinity.io account integration). When the operator runs `install.sh --mode tunnel --api-key liv_k_...` and the box boots, livinityd's start path arms a background heartbeat-sender. Every 60s it POSTs `{device_id, hostname, mode, version, ip, uptime, node_version}` to `https://livinity.io/api/devices/heartbeat` with `X-Api-Key: liv_k_...`. When Server5 ships the matching `/api/devices/heartbeat` route (separate v34.x repo work), Server5's `devices.last_seen` column updates and the "is your box online" dashboard widget lights up — with NO LivOS-side change needed (forward-compat). Three commits:
  1. `dc3d4044` `livos/packages/livinityd/source/modules/account/` (NEW directory, 9 files): api-key.ts (reads `/etc/livos/secrets/api-key` via Redis pointer key `livos:account:api_key_path` which 104-09 wrote; null on missing/empty/malformed; never throws; `redactedPreview()` helper produces `liv_k_XXXXXX***` for log lines — raw key NEVER on log surface), device-id.ts (stable per-box UUIDv4 via Node built-in `crypto.randomUUID()`, persists `/var/lib/livos/device-id` mode 0600; first call generates+persists, subsequent calls read; regenerates on malformed-file recovery), heartbeat-payload.ts (pure builder for ~200-byte JSON envelope), heartbeat-sender.ts (native fetch + 10s AbortController + self-rescheduling setTimeout chain so slow Server5 cannot cause tick pile-up + status matrix: 2xx verbose / 401 stop+error / 404 warn-once / 429 warn / 5xx warn / network err warn / returns stop() for graceful shutdown), index.ts (barrel export), + 4 vitest files (43 tests, 3.34s).
  2. `d5769318` `livos/packages/livinityd/source/index.ts` (Livinityd class wiring): new import `startHeartbeat, REDIS_KEY_API_KEY_PATH, type StopHandle as HeartbeatStopHandle`; new private field `stopHeartbeat?: HeartbeatStopHandle`; in `start()` AFTER `seedDefaultAliases()` block (Redis live, ai.start() done), guarded `await this.ai.redis.get(REDIS_KEY_API_KEY_PATH)` — armed only when 104-09 wrote the key, otherwise verbose-skip (no log spam for LAN-only installs); in `stop()` early `this.stopHeartbeat?.()` call before WebApp/Xvfb teardown so the setTimeout chain unwinds while redis+fetch are still healthy.
  3. `(this commit)` 104-10-SUMMARY.md + STATE.md + ROADMAP.md updates.
- Forward-compat with Server5 missing endpoint: until v34.x ships `/api/devices/heartbeat` route on Server5, POST returns 404. Sender logs a SINGLE warn line per livinityd restart (`warned404` flag) and downgrades subsequent 404s to verbose-level. When Server5 ships the route, the next POST lands and dashboards start working. NO LivOS-side code change required.
- D-104-RELAY-ZERO-DATA-PLANE compliance: heartbeat is CONTROL-PLANE traffic, explicitly allowed per the Phase 104 invariant. Envelope ~200 bytes, 60s interval → ~12KB/day. Three orders of magnitude smaller than the data-plane traffic the invariant actually targets. Documented in heartbeat-sender.ts top-of-file block + STATE comment. Data-plane (Master Chrome streams, agent payloads, file uploads, etc.) stays LAN-direct.
- Security: API key value NEVER logged in plaintext — only `redactedPreview()` form (`liv_k_<6-chars>***`); enforced by a dedicated test assertion that greps every captured log entry for the secret tail across the full happy-path POST flow. API key flows through HTTP `X-Api-Key` header only (never embedded in body); 10s `AbortController` timeout so a hung Server5 cannot leak fetch promises into livinityd.
- NO new npm deps: Node 18+ built-ins only — `fetch` (global), `AbortController` (global), `crypto.randomUUID` (`node:crypto`), `fs/promises`, `os`. `package.json` UNTOUCHED.
- Test coverage (43 tests): api-key.test.ts (12 — redactedPreview shape + safe-tail-length + happy path + all 5 null-return branches + Redis transient-error path + export-surface security guardrail), device-id.test.ts (8 — UUIDv4 shape + idempotent re-reads + malformed-file regeneration + recursive parent-dir mkdir + mode 0600 best-effort + statistical uniqueness + whitespace tolerance + chmod retry-path non-fatal), heartbeat-payload.test.ts (13 — full shape contract + every override + JSON serialization roundtrip + control-plane <1KB budget guard + IPv4 detection sanity), heartbeat-sender.test.ts (10 — happy path with header+body capture + 404 log-once + 401 stop+error + 5xx warn-retry + 429 warn-continue + network-err warn-continue + missing-api-key warn-once + stop() lifecycle + SECURITY raw-tail-never-logged + first-tick-after-interval).
- Sacred SHA `f3538e1d811992b782a9bb057d1b7f0a0189f95f` UNTOUCHED across all 3 commits (pre-commit hook gated every commit).
- Deviations: (1) Rule 1 — initial heartbeat-sender.test.ts used `vi.useFakeTimers()` with `vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync()` + `Promise.resolve()` microtask flushes to drive the self-rescheduling `setTimeout` chain. 9/10 tests failed because the microtask flush count was insufficient for the production code's `sendOnce()` await chain to complete before assertions fired. Fixed inline by rewriting all 10 tests to use REAL timers with a 50ms interval (`intervalSec: 0.05`) + `sleep()` waits — production code was correct; the test-side timing mock was the bug. (2) Rule 1 — `RequestInfo` global type not available in livinityd's `@tsconfig/node22` setup; typed test helper as `string | URL` to match what production sender actually passes. Both fixes inside Task 1's commit boundary — no broken interim form landed in git.
- Carry-forward to v34: when Server5 ships `/api/devices/heartbeat` route (separate repo, v34.x phase), no LivOS-side change required — existing deployed installs running 104-10 will automatically start updating `devices.last_seen` on the next heartbeat tick after Server5's endpoint goes live.

## 104-08 Status (2026-05-12) — user-owned-domain hybrid HOTFIX SHIPPED (18/18 host-side bash test PASS)

- **HOTFIX** (added to phase mid-flight after 104-07 Task 1): 104-08 adds `--domain` + `--cf-token` + `--cf-zone-id` flags + `LIVOS_*` env vars to install.sh. When supplied, `mode-hybrid.sh` skips the Server5 control-plane mint entirely and instead creates a Cloudflare DNS A-record on the user's own zone (idempotent list-first-then-create per T-104-04-R1). Realizes D-104-RELAY-ZERO-DATA-PLANE at install-time — for power users with their own domain, the entire Server5 touch is eliminated. Three commits:
  1. `3f8d20bc` parse-cli.sh (3 new CLI flags + 3 env-var bindings + partner-flag validation + --help rewrite with user-owned-domain bypass example + CGNAT limitation block) + detect-platform.sh (`detect_cgnat` advisory — RFC 6598 100.64.0.0/10 probe via ifconfig.me, WARN-not-FAIL semantics) + install.sh (1-line wire of `detect_cgnat`).
  2. `d9b2af27` mode-hybrid.sh (NEW `_provision_user_owned_domain` function + `LIVOS_DOMAIN`-non-empty early-exit in `_provision_hybrid_subdomain` + branch dispatch in `install_mode_hybrid` + `LIVOS_CF_TOKEN` fallback in `_write_cf_token_secret`) + show-banner.sh (user-owned-domain post-install URL + CGNAT advisory).
  3. `(Task-3 pending below)` __tests__/test-mode-hybrid-args.sh (NEW, executable, 18 assertions covering AC-104-08-{1..5} + bash -n syntax check + env-var equivalence) + 104-08-SUMMARY.md + STATE.md + ROADMAP.md.
- Security (AC-104-08-5): CF API token NEVER lands on curl argv. Uses `curl -K -` (config from stdin) for both GET (list-records) and POST (create-record) so `header = "Authorization: Bearer <token>"` flows via pipe, not argv. POST body is `mktemp` 0600 + `--data-binary @<file>` + `rm -f`. Body contains only DNS record payload, never the token. ## 103.1 Status (2026-05-11) — 5-LAYER FIX, fully live-verified on Mini PC

Bug 2 (list_windows aggregation) FULLY RESOLVED — user-walked verify 2026-05-11:
agent in global chat correctly enumerated 3 active displays (`:1`, `:11`, `:12`),
clicked into Dinkytown WebApp on `:12`, took screenshots and navigated
calculators end-to-end.

Bug 1 (master Chrome input) needed FIVE separate fixes (each surfaced by
re-running the live UAT). Layers A/B/C/D shipped in earlier 103.1-* commits.
Layer E (commit `3d9fe041`):

- **Symptom:** "klavyeye yaziyorum 'a' geç basıyor, delete çalışmıyor,
  mouse tıklamaları çalışmıyor".

- **Root cause:** Chrome detects `exited_cleanly:false` in Local State
  (legacy from prior livinityd restart) and pops a "Profile error occurred"
  modal. The modal is its own chrome-class top-level window with geometry
  ~400x213; fluxbox auto-focuses the last-opened window (the modal); the
  input dispatcher's `search --class chrome --limit 1 windowactivate` keeps
  re-picking the modal; every key/click lands on a non-input dialog and
  is dropped.

- **Fix:** post-spawn polling loop (`dismissProfileErrorAndActivateMain`)
  that (a) windowkills any "Profile error" window and (b) finds the
  largest-area chrome-class window and pre-activates it, so subsequent
  dispatch lands on the main Chrome browser. Awaited before startLogin
  returns so the wsUrl handed to the client points at a usable session.

Three-layer bug fix shipped and live-verified end-to-end via tRPC curl on
Mini PC at SHA `f3d471ac`:

- `startLogin` returned `{pid:1151469, display:":10", streamId:"bb999df0..."}`
- 10s post-spawn: `status.running:true` (daemonization filter survived
  the sudo wrapper code=0 exit)

- `hasCookies:true` (Chrome wrote to bruce-owned dir — chown succeeded)
- `ps -ef | grep google-chrome` → 2 processes alive
- Log shows `stream bb999df0 started` with NO subsequent `(stop requested)`
- `stopLogin` returns `{ok:true}` (clean shutdown)

Stale singleton locks were also verified cleaned (3 fake files I injected
earlier are gone after restartLogin).

## 103.1 Status (2026-05-11) — Hot-fix: stale singleton lock + chrome daemonization filter + list_windows cross-display aggregation

User-walked Phase 103 UAT on Mini PC (deployed SHA `c89f7139`) surfaced bugs not catchable by unit tests:

- **Bug 1A (stale singleton lock):** `clearStaleSingletonLocks()` removes `SingletonLock`/`SingletonCookie`/`SingletonSocket` before `chromeSpawnFn`. Commit `37f0bfb4`. Necessary but NOT sufficient.
- **Bug 1B (REAL WS 1006 cause — chrome daemonization):** `chromeMaster.startLogin` watched `chrome.child` (the `sudo google-chrome` wrapper). Chrome forks to background on startup; launcher exits with code=0. Pre-fix the exit handler treated ANY exit as a crash → `cleanupMaster` → `stopStream` → client WS 404 → browser code=1006. Fix: filter exit — `code=0+signal=null` → no-op (daemonization), only real crashes (`code!=0` or signal) trigger cleanup. Commit `e531b3c4`. Live-verified on Mini PC via tRPC curl: stream `e2462d48` got `(stop requested)` ms after start pre-1B-fix despite locks cleared. Bug 1 is a 2-cause stack (lock cleanup needed for refusal-to-start; daemonization filter needed for clean-spawn-survival).
- **Bug 2 (list_windows blind to other displays):** When neither display arg NOR defaultDisplay is set (global luse MCP, post-103-05 default-off model), aggregate across `/tmp/.X11-unix/X<N>` socket-scanned displays. Each result row carries its own `display` field. Commit `d634ffe4`.

Tests: 24/24 master-login (5 new including 15b daemonization + 15c signal-cleanup + 3 lock-cleanup) + 44/44 mcp tools (5 new). Sacred SHA `f3538e1d811992b782a9bb057d1b7f0a0189f95f` UNTOUCHED across all 4 commits (`37f0bfb4`, `d634ffe4`, `f8957fc0` docs, `e531b3c4`).

Pushed `c89f7139..e531b3c4` 2026-05-11. Mini PC re-deploy in flight.

Out of scope for 103.1 (deferred to a follow-on patch): duplicate x11vnc spawn cleanup in chromeMaster.startLogin (`vncSpawnFn` orphan, harmless but wasteful); active-WebApp roster prompt snippet (agent can discover via aggregation now); post-daemonization Chrome crash auto-detection via /proc PID polling (user recovers via "Close Master Chrome" button).

In parallel: research agent drafting `.planning/research/local-livinity-setup.md` for `<username>.livinity.local` domain-free local setup (next-phase 104+ scope).
